We can develop a gradient-based method to flatten b. Let a normalized sum=1. Objective=2n max b. The active peak set K = argmax b. We can move a in direction d that reduces max at active peaks while preserving sum and nonnegativity. For a small perturbation δ (sum zero), change in b_k ≈2 a^T Q_k δ =2 g_k·δ, where g^{(k)} as above. We want for active k: g_k·δ ≤0. To reduce max, choose δ = -∑ active g_k projected to sum-zero and scaled, with clipping. This is a steepest-descent subgradient. We can do multiple steps. This is more directed than random. Also use smooth max with weights w_k ∝ exp(β b_k /b0) to address near peaks.

Because a nonnegative, when updating do a ← a - η d, clip to 0, renormalize. Need step size. We can compute direction and use line search: try several η (or exact scale to reduce active b). Use finite improvement. Since b quadratic, direction can be computed fast via cross-correlation of w with a.

Algorithm subgradient/SGD:
- n choose 1024 or 2048.
- a init from best of param/random.
- Normalize sum=1.
- Compute b via fftconvolve.
- For iteration until time:
   * smooth weights w: sort b or logsumexp. β maybe adaptive: start high? If β large, only max; if low flatten. Use B = max(b) small ~0.5/n? Actually b values ~e/n ~0.75/n ~0.0004; β*n maybe. To make weights meaningful, use scale = b_avg or b_max. weights = exp(β*(b - max_b)/scale). scale = max_b or mean positive. β from 5 to 50? Since b variable range maybe O(max). Use β in [10,100].
   * Compute d_i = ∑_r a_r w_{i+r} (odd cross-correlation) as above (without factor2). This points to increase b at weighted peaks. We want decrease, so sd = -d. Remove mean? To normalize sum, adjust d -= mean(d) (or project on sum-zero). Actually if we change a and renormalize, sum change absorbed; to avoid scale, can subtract a * (a·d)/? For softmax direction. Simpler: d = -d + mean(d) (sum zero). Step with multiplicative update? Since a nonnegative, use relative step: a_new = a * exp(-η d/A) maybe. But d may have negative. Use a_new = a * (1 + η * d/a?) no. Let's use log-space direction: a_i ∝ exp(θ_i). Gradient wrt θ earlier: gradθ_j = a_j d_j - a_j a·d, where d_j=2∑ w a_{k-j}. We can use negative grad: η (a·d - a_j d_j) and update θ += η grad. But can approximate directly on a via exponentials.
   * Or use projected vector direction: δ = α d0, where d0 = -d + mean(d). Choose α via line search such that max b decreases. Because b quadratic, we can compute b(δ) fast? For each candidate α, a=normalize(a+α d0). Could try 5 alphas, evaluate b, choose best. This is coordinate-free and robust.

   * Use block multiplicative random perturbations to escape.

L-BFGS objective:
```
def _lbfbs_objective(theta, n, beta, scale=2.0, max_b maybe):
    t = theta - np.max(theta)
    a = np.exp(t)
    a /= a.sum()
    b = self_convolution(a)
    bm = b.max()
    if bm <=0: return 0, zeros
    centered = b - bm
    # soft max with beta scaled; beta dimension? b values ~e/n. To get meaningful weights, betaea = beta / max(bm,1e-12)? Then exp(betaea*centered) where centered≤0 range [-bm,0]. Use betaea = beta / max(bm,1e-12) -> exponent in [-beta,0]. Good.
    ew = np.exp(betaea*centered)
    w = ew / ew.sum()
    val = 2*n * (bm + np.log(ew.sum())/betaea)
    d = cross_corr_a_w(a,w)
    H = 2*d # derivative of softval? Since val=2n(Lβ), derivative=2n*2d=4n d? Let's compute val = 2n Lβ, Lβ derivative wrt a=2d. dJ/da=4n d. Then θ grad = a*(4n d_j -4n a_d) = 4n a(d_j-a_d). But we can scale gradient by constant; L-BFGS can handle. For stability, divide by 4n? Since objective scale can be removed; use val = Lβ (no 2n) or return value=2nL but grad scaled down. L-BFGS uses gradient magnitude; scaling matters. We can return value=2nL, grad = a*(d_j - a_d) * factor 4n? Actually gradient of 2nL is 4n a(d-a_d). If we return grad /4n = a(d-a_d), value still 2nL, inconsistent but L-BFGS only uses grad direction and line search? It uses grad for step direction but value separate; scale mismatch might affect step length. Better return value=Lβ and grad=a(d-a_d), then convert final score =2n*actual max. But L-BFGS minimizes Lβ, equivalent to max for high beta. We'll use objective internal val=Lβ (or normalized by n) to avoid large constants. Final score not used by optimizer. In L-BFGS, scale irrelevant if consistent. Let's return internal = Lβ (or Lβ * 2n?). If internal = Lβ, grad=a(d-a_d) (since dJ/da=2d -> θ grad=2a(d-a_d); factor2 can ignore). Use grad = a*(d-a_d). This is gradient of L? L derivative 2d, θ grad=2a(d-a_d). Factor 2 ignored. Good. Return val = Lβ? For final not. OK.
    grad = 2*a*(d - np.dot(a,d))? Since ascent gradient. But if return val=Lβ, gradient should match derivative: dθ L = 2a(d-a_d). Use grad=2a(d-a_d). Factor not critical but let's correct.
    grad -= grad.mean()
    return internal_val, grad
```
But note b is length 2n-1, Lβ approx max(b). Objective target score=2n max(b). If internal val difference, fine.

Use beta: Start with beta=10? Since b max ~1/n~0.0005, betaea=beta/bm gives exponent [-beta,0], beta=10 moderate; high beta=50 for sharp. Anneal beta: maybe run L-BFGS with beta=10, then 50? But if internal val Lβ, fresh min. High beta nonconvex. Use beta=10 then 50.

Let's implement a function _initial_candidates(n, seed, time_limit) that returns list of normalized arrays and scores:
1. uniform with small linear tilt: a = 1+α*(2x-1) + noise? ensure positive; α=0.4 and random signs.
2. sin2πx family with random coefficients:
   x = np.linspace(0,1,n)
   for several (ρ1,ρ2,ρ3) random in [-0.9,0.9] with constraints p≥0. Use p=1 + c1 sin(2πx)+c2 sin(4πx)+c3 sin(6πx)+c4 sin(8πx)+c5 sin(10πx). Normalize. We can sample random coefficients and clip negative; but clipping may distort. Use p=exp(v) where v = sum h_m sin(2πm x + φ) with decaying amplitudes, to ensure positive. This gives smooth shapes. ```
def local_search(a, deadline, seed):
    a=normalize(a)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score(a)
    cur_a=a.copy(); cur_score=best_score
    rng=default_rng(seed)
    # precompute x for block? not.
    no_improve=0
    while now < deadline:
        # alternating: smooth subgradient step (many p)
        for mode in range(...):
            now check
            if rng.random()<0.2: block perturb
            elif rng.random()<0.2: coordinate/random
            else: subgradient with p chosen randomly (maybe low p if score high, high if stuck)
            evaluate candidate, accept via anneal.
        # if no improve 500 steps, restart current from best with random perturbation.
```

Subgradient multiplicative update:
```
def subgradient_step(a, p, t):
    b=self_conv(a); current=score
    bm=b.max(); if bm<=0 return a
    w=(b/bm)**p; w/=w.sum()
    d=cross_corr(a,w)
    mean_d=d.mean(); dir=mean_d - d
    scale=dir.max(abs())
    if scale==0: return a
    for step in steps[t]:
        loga=np.log(a+1e-300) + step*(dir/scale)
        cand=np.exp(loga); cand/=cand.sum()
        sc=score(cand)
        if best: return cand
    return a
```

def fourier_candidate(n, coeffs):
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    v=0
    # use odd harmonics of period1? sin(2π m x). Nonnegative via exp.

    p=np.exp(v - np.mean(v))
    p *=1/p.sum()

```
def cross_corr_a_w(a,w):
    n=len(a); L=1; while L < 3*n: L <<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w,L)
    d=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(fa)*fw, L)
    return d[:n]
```

For self convolution via FFT:
```
def self_convolution(a):
    n=len(a)
    if n <= 128: return np.convolve(a,a)
    L=1; while L < 2*n: L <<=1 # at least 2n-1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, L)
    return c[:2*n-1]
```

Let's design `_fourier_array`:
```
def _fourier_array(n, rng, kind=0):
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    if kind==0: # tilted uniform
       alpha=float(rng.uniform(0.2,0.9)); sign=rng.choice([-1,1]); p=1+alpha*sign*(2*x-1); p=np.clip(p,1e-6,None); p/=p.sum(); return p
    if kind==1: # Fourier exp
       v=0.0
       K=int(rng.integers(3,8))
       for m in range(1,K+1):
          amp=float(rng.uniform(0.02, 1.2/(m+0.5)))
          phase=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
          v += amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*m*x+phase)
       v -= v.mean()
       p=np.exp(v)
       p/=p.sum(); return p
    if kind==2: # low-pass random cosine scaled direct
       v=... cos maybe
    if kind==3: # power law asym p=(x+eps)^a + small
```
Ensure p≥0 before returning. For direct p may become negative if using 1+...; only use exp.

```
def _local_search(a, seed, deadline, verbose=True):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    a=_normalize(a)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=_score(a)
    cur=a.copy(); cur_score=best_score
    steps=0; no_improve=0; T=0.01
    # constants
    p_options=[1,2,3,5,8,13,21,34]
    steps_list=[0.005,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,1.0]
    factor_list=[0.3,0.7,1.4,2.0,0.0]
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        steps +=1
        if steps % 200 ==0: print best score, cur score
        # choose move type
        u=rng.random()
        if u < 0.55:
            # smooth gradient step
            p = p_options[min(len-1, int(rng.random()*len))] maybe schedule
            cand = _smooth_candidate(cur, p, rng, steps_list)
            sc = _score(cand)
        elif u < 0.75:
            cand = _block_candidate(cur, rng, p? maybe)
            sc = _score(cand)
        else:
            cand = _coord_candidate(cur, rng, factor_list)
            sc = _score(cand)
        if sc < cur_score - 1e-12:
            cur=cand; cur_score=sc; no_improve=0
            if sc < best_score:
                best=cand.copy(); best_score=sc; T=max(1e-6, T*0.98)
            T=max(1e-6, T*0.99)
        else:
            no_improve +=1
            accept = (np.log(rng.random()+1e-12) > (cur_score - sc)/T) # since new worse: sc-cur>0, accept if log< (cur-sc)/T
            if accept and sc < cur_score + 0.05? maybe:
                cur=cand; cur_score=sc
        if no_improve > 300:
            # restart from best
            cur = _perturb_log(best, 0.1, rng)
            cur_score = _score(cur)
            no_improve=0
            T=0.005
    return best,best_score
```

LP phase low-res:
```
def _lp_candidate(a):
   n=len(a); a=_normalize(a)
   b=_self_convolution(a); M=b.max()
   if M<=0: return None
   m=2*n-1
   T=np.zeros((m,n))
   for x in range(m):
       start=max(0,x-n+1); stop=min(n-1,x)
       if stop>=start:
          idx=start+np.arange(stop-start+1)
          T[x, idx] = a[x - idx]
   try:
      res=opt.linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=T, b_ub=M, bounds=[(0,None)]*n, method='highs')
      if res.success:
          g=res.x
          s=g.sum()
          if s>1e-12:
             g/=s
             return normalize(g)
   except: pass
   return None
```

Implementation of `_lbfbs_func`:
```
def _lbfbs_func(theta, n, beta):
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(theta)):
        return 1e6, np.zeros(n)
    t=theta - theta.max()
    a=np.exp(t)
    s=a.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <=1e-300:
        return 1e6, np.zeros(n)
    a/=s
    b=_self_convolution(a)
    bm=b.max()
    if bm <=0:
       val=0.0
       grad=np.zeros(n)
       return val, grad
    beta_scaled = beta / bm
    centered=b - bm
    ew=np.exp(beta_scaled*centered)
    ew_sum=ew.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(ew_sum) or ew_sum<=0:
        val=2*n*bm? internal? 
        w=np.zeros(2*n-1); ...
    w=ew/ew_sum
    internal = bm + np.log(ew_sum)/beta_scaled
    # For optimizer, constant 2*n doesn't matter, but return 2*n*internal? We can return 2*n*internal for matching target lower. Gradient scaling maybe huge; L-BFGS ok? grad = 2*a*(d - dot(a,d)) * 2*n? Let's return value = 2*n*internal, grad = 4*n * a*(d - dot) to be correct. But large constants ~1000; L-BFGS maybe scaled. Better return value=internal (or normalized by n) and grad=a*(d-dot) (or 2a). Since final shape same. I'll return value = internal (not 2n) to small; grad = 2*a*(d - dot). Factor arbitrary but consistent.
    d=_cross_corr_a_w(a,w)
    ad=np.dot(a,d)
    grad=2.0*a*(d - ad)
    grad -= grad.mean()
    return internal, grad
```
If beta high and bm small, beta_scaled=beta/bm, centered/bm in [-1,0], ew fine. `internal` approx min? For uniform n=256, bm~0.0039? internal ~0.0039. Good. L-BFGS minimizes bm approximate.
But objective for final is 2n*bm ~2. Internal differences 0.001; L-BFGS ftol1e-12 okay.

Potential bug: `_cross_corr_a_w` w length 2n-1, a length n. For n=256, L while L < 3*n =768 ->1024. Good. But if w nonzero only up to 2n-2; circular terms w_{m+j} for m up to n-1, j up to n-1 need up to2n-2; L≥2n-1 enough? But if m+j = L? max 2n-2, L=2n? If L=2n exactly for self, but here L=1024 for n=256 >=768, safe.

3. `_self_convolution`:
```
def _self_convolution(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    if n ==0: return np.array([])
    if n <= 128:
        c=np.convolve(a,a)
        c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
        return c
    L=1
    need=2*n-1
    while L < need:
        L <<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, L)
    c=c[:need]
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    return c
```
Using need=2*n-1; L>=need. In self conv, if L==2n? For n=256, need511, L512. Good.

def _score(a):

    s=a.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <=1e-300: return inf
    if abs(s-1)>1e-10:
       a=a/s
    else:
       # ensure nonnegative finite? maybe
       a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    b=_self_convolution(a)
    if b.size==0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(b)): return inf
    return float(2.0*a.size*np.max(b))
```

def _cross_corr_a_w(a,w):
    n=len(a)
    if n==0: return np.array([])
    L=1
    need=3*n-2  # safe for indices
    while L < need:
       L <<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w, L)
    d=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(fa)*fw, L)
    d=d[:n]
    # due to numerical, maybe clean
    d=np.maximum? It should be nonnegative if a,w nonnegative? Cross sum nonnegative. Use np.clip(d,0,None)
    return d
```

6. `_fourier_array`: as above. Need ensure output sum1 and finite.
```
def _fourier_array(n, rng, mode):
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    if mode==0:
       alpha=float(rng.uniform(-0.9,0.9))
       p=1.0 + alpha*(2*x-1)
    elif mode==1:
       v=0
       K=int(rng.integers(3,8))
       for m in range(1,K+1):
          amp=float(rng.uniform(0.0,1.0/(m+0.3)))
          phase=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
          v += amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*m*x+phase)
       v -= v.mean()
       # optionally scale to control dynamic range
       sd=v.std()
       if sd>0: v = v * float(rng.uniform(0.5,1.5)) / sd? maybe.
       p=np.exp(v)
    elif mode==2:
       v=... cos
    elif mode==3:
       alpha=float(rng.uniform(0.2,1.5)); eps=0.01; p=(x+eps)**alpha + 0.05*(1-x)**(1/alpha)?
    else: p=np.ones(n)
    p=np.clip(p,1e-8,None)
    p/=p.sum()
    return p
```
For mode0, alpha can make negative if >1; clip to1e-8.
For mode1, `v -= v.mean()` then if sd>0: `v = float(rng.uniform(0.3,1.2))*v/sd` to make standard deviation ~1; ensures nontrivial. Then p=exp(v). Could be peak ratios exp(1)~2.7, okay. Use `scale=float(rng.uniform(0.2,1.2))` so p moderate.
For mode3, asymmetric power: `a=float(rng.uniform(0.3,3.0)); eps=0.01; p=(x+eps)**a + 0.2*(1-x+eps)**a`? Need nonnegative. Or p=(x+0.01)**a * (1-x+0.01)**b with random. Use exp maybe enough.

8. `_resample_sequence(a, n)`:
```
def _resample_sequence(a,n):
    a=_normalize(a)
    if a.size == n: return a
    if a.size==0: return np.ones(n)/n
    loga=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
    x0=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,a.size)
    x1=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    li=np.interp(x1,x0,loga)
    out=np.exp(li-li.max())
    out/=out.sum()
    return out
```
If a.size==1: x0 [0], interp all same; out uniform? okay.

9. `_lp_blend_candidate(a, max_calls=1)`? Need top-level, no closure.
```
def _lp_update(gmaybe): ...
```
Use scipy if available; but linprog inside can be slow if n>300. So only use if n<=256.
```
def _lp_candidate(a):
    if not HAVE_SCIPY: return None
    a=_normalize(a); n=a.size
    if n>300: return None
    b=_self_convolution(a); M=float(np.max(b))
    if M <=0: return None
    m=2*n-1
    T=np.zeros((m,n), dtype=np.float64)
    for x in range(m):
       start=max(0,x-n+1); stop=min(n-1,x)
       if stop >= start:
          idx=np.arange(start,stop+1)
          T[x, idx] = a[x - idx]
    try:
       res=opt.linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=T, b_ub=M, bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n, method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
       if res.success:
          g=res.x
          s=g.sum()
          if s > 1e-12:
            return _normalize(g)
    except Exception:
       pass
    return None
```
Potential memory if n=300: 599*300=180k, fine. `bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n` uses tuple list, no lambda.
Could `linprog` be unavailable method? We can try default. If method='highs' unsupported, exception.
Time: dense T build O(n^2). Good.

`_lp_blend_candidate(a)`:
```
def _lp_blend_candidate(a):
    a=_normalize(a)
    base_sc=_score(a)
    best=(a, base_sc)
    g=_lp_candidate(a)
    if g is None: return best
    for t in (0.1,0.3,0.5,0.7,1.0):
       cand=(1-t)*a + t*g
       cand=_normalize(cand)
       sc=_score(cand)
       if sc < best[1]: best=(cand,sc)
    return best
```

10. `_smooth_candidate(a, p, step_values, rng)`:
```
def _smooth_candidate(a, p, step_values, rng):
    a=_normalize(a)
    base_sc=_score(a)
    best=(a,base_sc)
    b=_self_convolution(a)
    bm=b.max()
    if bm <= 1e-300:
       return best
    y=b/bm
    y=np.maximum(y,0.0)
    w=np.power(y, float(p))
    sw=w.sum()
    if sw <=0 or not finite: return best
    w=w/sw
    d=_cross_corr_a_w(a,w)
    if d.size==0: return best
    mean_d=float(np.mean(d))
    direction=mean_d - d
    scale=float(np.max(np.abs(direction)))
    if scale < 1e-15: return best
    # choose a subset of steps maybe all
    for t in step_values:
        loga=np.log(a + 1e-300) + float(t) * (direction / scale)
        cand=np.exp(loga - loga.max())
        sc = cand.sum()
        if sc > 1e-300:
           cand=cand/sc
        else: cand=_normalize(a)
        sc=_score(cand)
        if sc < best[1]: best=(cand,sc)
    return best
```
This scores a again; to reduce, accept base_sc variable. But base_sc computed. Good.

Step values: For local high, maybe [0.01,0.05,0.1,0.3,1.0]. For low, maybe [0.02,0.1,0.3,1.0,3.0]. Use parameter.

11. `_block_candidate(a, rng)`:
```
def _block_candidate(a, rng):
    a=_normalize(a); cand=a.copy(); n=a.size
    # choose width category
    u=rng.random()
    if u<0.3:
       width=int(rng.integers(2, max(3, min(n, 16))))
       sigma=float(rng.uniform(0.02,0.2))
    elif u<0.7:
       width=int(rng.integers(max(3,n//20), max(4,n//5)))
       sigma=float(rng.uniform(0.03,0.25))
    else:
       width=int(rng.integers(max(4,n//10), max(5,n//2)))
       sigma=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.5))
    if width>=n: start=0; width=n
    else: start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width+1))
    deltas=rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=width)
    cand[start:start+width] *= np.exp(deltas)
    if rng.random()<0.2:
       # zero a small left/right? maybe choose fraction
       zwidth=max(1,width//4)
       zstart=start + int(rng.integers(0, max(1,width-zwidth+1)))
       cand[zstart:zstart+zwidth] = 0.0
    cand=_normalize(cand)
    return cand, _score(cand)
```
Width n for full multiply; no effect if full uniform but okay.

12. `_coord_candidate(a, rng)`:
```
def _coord_candidate(a, rng):
    a=_normalize(a); cand=a.copy(); n=a.size
    idx=int(rng.integers(0,n))
    factors=(0.0,0.2,0.5,0.8,1.25,1.6,2.5,5.0)
    f=float(rng.choice(factors))
    cand[idx] *= f
    cand=_normalize(cand)
    return cand, _score(cand)
```
Maybe if factor0 and idx only nonzero? sum zero? normalize fallback.

13. `_perturb_log(a, sigma, rng)`:

loga=np.log(_normalize(a)+1e-300)
loga += rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=loga.size)
out=np.exp(loga - loga.max())
return _normalize(out)
```

def _local_search(a, seed, deadline, allow_lp=False, lp_interval=400, lp_max=5):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    a=_normalize(a)
    cur=a.copy(); cur_sc=_score(cur)
    best=a.copy(); best_sc=cur_sc
    steps=0; no_improve=0; lp_calls=0
    T=0.01
    step_values=(0.01,0.05,0.1,0.3,1.0)
    p_options=(1,2,3,5,8,13,21,34)
    low_t = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       steps +=1
       if lp_calls < lp_max and allow_lp and steps % max(1, lp_interval)==0:
           cand, sc = _lp_blend_candidate(cur)
       else:
           u=rng.random()
           if u < 0.55:
               # choose p with schedule: if best_sc > 1.7 broadish, else sharp
               if best_sc > 1.7:
                   p = int(p_options[int(rng.integers(0, len(p_options)//2))])
               else:
                   p = int(p_options[int(rng.integers(len(p_options)//2, len(p_options)))])
               cand_sc = _smooth_candidate(cur, p, step_values, rng)
           elif u < 0.8:
               cand_sc = _block_candidate(cur, rng)
           else:
               cand_sc = _coord_candidate(cur, rng)
       cand=sc0? Actually _smooth_candidate returns tuple (cand, sc), block/coord tuple.
       sc = cand_sc[1]
       if not finite: continue
       if sc < cur_sc - 1e-12:
          cur=cand; cur_sc=sc; no_improve=0
          if sc < best_sc:
             best=cand.copy(); best_sc=sc
             T=max(1e-6, T*0.98)
          T=max(1e-6, T*0.995)
       else:
          no_improve +=1
          if T > 1e-8:
             diff=cur_sc - sc  # negative if worse? if sc > cur, diff negative; accept if log(u) < (cur-sc)/T? Since sc-cur positive.
             # accept_prob = exp(-(sc-cur)/T)
             if rng.random() < np.exp(-max(0.0, sc - cur_sc)/T):
                cur=cand; cur_sc=sc
          # no best update
       if steps % 1000 ==0:
          print("local step", steps, "best", best_sc, "cur", cur_sc, flush=True)
       if no_improve > 400:
          cur=_perturb_log(best, 0.08, rng)
          cur_sc=_score(cur)
          no_improve=0
          T=0.004
          if steps % 1000 ==0: print restart
    if allow_lp: lp_calls? set global? no; lp_calls local.
    return best, best_sc

15. `_optimize_with_lbfbs(a, seed, deadline, max_candidates=2, betas=(10,40), n=None)`:

def _lbfbs_polish(a, n, beta, maxfun, maxiter):
    a0=_normalize(a)
    theta=np.log(a0+1e-300); theta-=theta.mean()
    if not HAVE_SCIPY: return a0
    args=(n,beta)
    try:
       res=opt.minimize(_lbfbs_func, theta, args=(n,beta), jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxfun,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-8})
       if res.x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
           th=res.x - res.x.max()
           out=np.exp(th); out/=out.sum(); _normalize(out)
       else out=a0
    except Exception: out=a0
    return out
```

def _make_initial_candidates(n, seed, parent_seq=None, max_candidates=80):

`_fourier_array` mode count: 5 modes. Modes:
0: tilted linear.
1: exp random sine harmonics.
2: exp random cos harmonics (maybe asymmetric phase).
3: product power: p=(x+eps)^alpha * (1-x+eps)^beta; with alpha,beta random in [-?]? If negative exponents cause singular; use alpha,rng.uniform(-0.5,3) and beta. Clip because x=0 singular if negative. If exponent negative, p large endpoints; may be bad but explore. Use eps=0.02.
4: mixture: p = 0.5 + 0.5*exp(v) with v low? Or smooth positive direct.
Let's implement:
```
def _fourier_array(n, rng, mode):
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    if mode ==0:
        alpha=float(rng.uniform(-0.8,0.8))
        p=1.0+alpha*(2*x-1)
    elif mode ==1:
        K=int(rng.integers(3,8))
        v=np.zeros(n)
        for m in range(1,K+1):
            amp=float(rng.uniform(0.0,1.0/(m+0.4)))
            ph=float(rng.uniform(0.0,2.0*np.pi))
            v += amp*np.sin(2.0*np.pi*m*x+ph)
        sd=float(np.std(v))
        if sd>1e-12:
            v *= float(rng.uniform(0.3,1.5))/sd
        else:
            v = rng.normal(0,0.5,size=n)
        p=np.exp(v)
    elif mode ==2:
        K=int(rng.integers(3,8))
        v=np.zeros(n)
        for m in range(1,K+1):
           amp=... ; ph=...
           v += amp*np.cos(2πm x+ph)
        normalize std similarly; p=1+0.7*np.tanh(v)? To ensure nonneg? p=np.exp(v)
    elif mode ==3:
        alpha=float(rng.uniform(-0.4,3.0)); beta=float(rng.uniform(-0.4,3.0)); eps=0.02
        p=(x+eps)**alpha * (1.0-x+eps)**beta
    else:
        # low-pass positive with two tones in log
        A1=float(rng.uniform(0.2,1.0)); A2=float(rng.uniform(0.1,0.6)); ph1=rng.uniform(0,2π); ph2=rng.uniform(0,2π); f1=rng.integers(1,4); f2=rng.integers(2,8)
        p=1.0 + A1*np.sin(2π f1 x / n? use 2πf1 x) + A2*np.sin(2π f2 x+ph2)
```
For mode 4, p may go negative; clip to 1e-8. It may have zeros, okay.
Mode2 maybe p=exp(v) but v random cos might have large p; okay.

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=400.0, **kwargs):
    start=time.monotonic()
    total=float(budget_s) if budget_s is not None else 400.0
    safety=5.0
    total_time=max(1.0, total - safety)
    deadline=start+total_time
    try: parent=height_sequence_1
    except Exception: parent=None
    # if parent not list/arr?
    n_low=256; n_high=1024
    low_budget_min = min(70.0, 0.25*total_time)
    low_deadline=start+low_budget_min
    # If budget very small, maybe skip low and direct high
    best_a=None; best_sc=inf
    if parent is None:
       parent_arr=None
    else:
       parent_arr=np.asarray(parent)
       if parent_arr.ndim !=1: parent_arr=parent_arr.ravel()
       if parent_arr.size==0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(parent_arr)): parent_arr=None
    if total_time > 30:
        # low search
        low_a = _low_res_search(n_low, int(seed), low_deadline, parent_arr)
        if low_a is not None:
            high_a = _resample_sequence(low_a, n_high)
            sc = _score(high_a)
            best_a=high_a; best_sc=sc
            print("low score", ...)
    # direct high initial candidates
    high_inits = _make_initial_candidates(n_high, int(seed)+777, parent_arr, max_candidates=60)
    for sc,a in high_inits[:5]:
       if sc < best_sc:
          best_a=a; best_sc=sc
    if best_a is None:
       best_a=np.ones(n_high)/n_high; best_sc=_score(best_a)
    print initial best
    # high local
    high_deadline=deadline
    # If best score is very high, use more aggressive local? _local_search knows.
    best_a,best_sc=_local_search(best_a, int(seed)+1234, high_deadline, allow_lp=False, lp_interval=0)
    print high local best
    # optional lbfbs polish high if time left
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 40:
       pol=_lbfbs_polish(best_a, n_high, 40, 1500, 300)
       sc=_score(pol)
       if sc < best_sc: best=pol; best_sc=sc
       # then short local if remains
       if time.monotonic() < deadline - 20:
          best_a,best_sc=_local_search(best_a, seed+4321, min(deadline, time.monotonic()+30), allow_lp=False)
    # optional larger n if still not close and time left

       a2,sc2=_local_search(a2, seed+555, dl2, allow_lp=False)
       if sc2 < best_sc:
          best_a=a2; best_sc=sc2
    # final validate with evaluate_sequence

    try:
       exact=evaluate_sequence(final_list)
       print("exact evaluate", exact)
       if not np.isfinite(exact):
          # fallback uniform
          final_list=[1.0]/? no, need sum? evaluator handles; uniform length1024.
          final_list=[1.0/1024.0]*1024
    except Exception:
       pass
    # Ensure no negative/inf; maybe clip
    return final_list
```

Now, L-BFGS objective with n=1024 and beta=40: beta_scaled=40/bm; centered range -bm, exponent [-40,0]; exp underflow to0 for far below max, okay. w sum maybe small? For many below max underflow, sum maybe only few active; fine. If beta=40, smooth approximates max. Good. For beta=10, smoother.

`_lbfbs_func` returns internal val not scaled by n; L-BFGS may stop due ftol when internal changes tiny (1e-12). But may need many. `gftol` maybe. Options `gtol=1e-7`. Good.

Potential issue: L-BFGS with many variables and gradient zero flat direction: we subtract mean; but objective invariant to adding constant to theta? Our function first centers by max, but theta + c yields same a; gradient after subtract mean maybe not exactly zero due numerical. L-BFGS may still step along constant if gradients not zero? It might confuse. Could reduce flat direction by fixing last coordinate? Another way: parameterize theta with sum zero: In `_lbfbs_func`, before compute, `t = theta - theta.mean()` to enforce zero mean. Gradient should be projected to sum zero: `grad -= grad.mean()`. But if we change theta by modified projection, objective sees only projected part; L-BFGS may still take steps in constant direction because objective flat, gradient zero, okay. But if we modify theta in function (not in variable), the Hessian approximations in L-BFGS assume original theta; could be messed by flat direction. Better to use a constrained parameterization with n-1 dimensions: let x length n-1, define theta_i = x_i for i=0..n-2, theta_{n-1} = -sum_{0..n-2} x_i (so sum zero). Then no flat direction. But we need map x (n-1) to theta. Function takes x. Let's implement! It adds complexity but better.

th = np.empty(n)

Function `_lbfbs_func_x(x, n, beta)`:
- th=desired; a=exp(th-th.max)/sum.
- compute d, ad, grad_theta = 2a(d-ad) (as before) with respect to theta. Then gradient with respect to x: ∂θ/∂x is n x (n-1): identity for first n-1; last row -ones. So grad_x_i = grad_theta_i - grad_theta_{n-1} for i<n-1; grad_x_{n-1}? x length n-1, gradient length n-1: grad_x[i]=grad_theta[i] - grad_theta[-1]. This projects onto sum-zero space. Return internal val, grad_x. Great.
This removes flat direction and reduces dimension by 1. Use x initial length n-1 from theta centered first n-1.
`_lbfbs_polish` must use x.
```
th= log(a)-mean; x=th[:n-1] (sum might not be -th[n-1]? If sum th=0, th[n-1]= -sum first; yes)
res=opt.minimize(_lbfbs_func_x, x, args=(n,beta), ...)
th = _theta_from_x(res.x); normalize exp.
```

`_lbfbs_func_x`:
```
def _lbfbs_func_x(x, n, beta):
    if x.size != n-1: ...
    th=_theta_from_x(x,n)
    if not finite(th): return 1e6, np.zeros(n-1)
    m=th.max()
    a=np.exp(th-m)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return 1e6, zeros
    a/=s
    b=_self_convolution(a)
    bm=b.max()
    if bm<=0: val=0; d=zeros? grad zeros; return val, zeros
    lam = beta / bm
    ew=np.exp(lam*(b-bm))
    sw=ew.sum()
    if sw<=0 or not finite:
       # fallback w one-hot at max
       w=np.zeros(b.size); idx=int(np.argmax(b)); w[idx]=1; ew=??? internal=bm
       internal=bm
       d=_cross_corr_a_w(a,w)
       ad=np.dot(a,d)
       grad_th=2*a*(d-ad)
       grad_x=grad_th[:-1] - grad_th[-1]
       return internal, grad_x
    w=ew/sw
    internal = bm + np.log(sw)/lam
    d=_cross_corr_a_w(a,w)
    ad=float(np.dot(a,d))
    grad_th=2.0*a*(d-ad)
    grad_x=grad_th[:-1] - grad_th[-1]
    # sanitize
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_x)): grad_x=np.zeros(n-1)
    return float(internal), grad_x
```
This gradient is for internal (smooth max). Good.

One subtlety: a = softmax(th), sum1. When θ sum zero, a insensitive to constant, but fine.

`_lbfbs_polish`:
```
def _lbfbs_polish(a, n, beta, maxfun, maxiter):
    a=_normalize(a)
    if n<=1: return a
    th=np.log(a+1e-300); th-=th.mean()
    x=th[:n-1]
    if not HAVE_SCIPY: return a
    try:
       res=opt.minimize(_lbfbs_func_x, x, args=(n,beta), jac=True, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxfun,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-7})
       if res.x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
           th=_theta_from_x(res.x,n)
           th=th-th.max()
           out=np.exp(th)
           s=out.sum()
           if s>0 and np.isfinite(s): out=out/s; out=np.clip(out,0,None); return _normalize(out)
    except Exception:
       pass
    return a
```

```
def _low_res_search(n, seed, deadline, parent_seq=None):
    inits=_make_initial_candidates(n, seed, parent_seq, max_candidates=80)
    if len(inits)==0:
       return np.ones(n)/n
    best_a=inits[0][1]; best_sc=inits[0][0]
    print("low initial best", best_sc)
    # LP initial
    if HAVE_SCIPY and n <= 200:
       cand_sc=_lp_blend_candidate(best_a)
       if cand_sc[1] < best_sc:
          best_a=cand_sc[0]; best_sc=cand_sc[1]
    # L-BFGS top 2
    top_count=min(2, len(inits))
    for idx in range(top_count):
       init_a=inits[idx][1]
       for beta in (8,25,60):
          if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 5:
             break
          maxfun = 5000 if n <= 160 else 2000
          maxiter = 700
          polished=_lbfbs_polish(init_a, n, beta, maxfun, maxiter)
          sc=_score(polished)
          if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
             best_a=polished; best_sc=sc
          print("low lbfgs beta", beta, "score", sc)
    # local search until deadline
    a,sc=_local_search(best_a, seed+99999, deadline, allow_lp=(HAVE_SCIPY and n <= 200), lp_interval=200, lp_max=6)
    print("low local best", sc)
    return a
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a)^2).

Strategy:
- Work with sequences normalized to sum 1, so the score is 2*n*max(conv(a,a)).
- Use a low-resolution phase (n=128) with smooth L-BFGS optimization, a few
  LP-based updates, and noisy local search to find a good macroscopic shape.
- Upsample the best low-resolution shape to a higher resolution (n=1024, and
  optionally n=2048 if time remains) and refine with FFT-based local search.
- The local search combines:
    * smooth subgradient/mirror-descent steps perturbing the log-weights,
    * block multiplicative perturbations,
    * coordinate perturbations,
    * simulated annealing and restarts.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    import scipy.optimize as _opt
    _HAVE_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    _HAVE_SCIPY = False


def _as_1d(x):
    arr = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.ndim != 1:
        arr = arr.ravel()
    return arr


def _normalize(a):
    a = _as_1d(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def _self_convolution(a):
    a = _as_1d(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 128:
        c = np.convolve(a, a)
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        return c
    need = 2 * n - 1
    L = 1
    while L < need:
        L <<= 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, L)
    c = c[:need]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def _score(a):
    a = _as_1d(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return float("inf")
    if abs(s - 1.0) > 1e-10:
        a = a / s
    b = _self_convolution(a)
    if b.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(b)):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * a.size * float(np.max(b)))


def _cross_corr_a_w(a, w):
    """
    Returns d where
        d[i] = sum_{r=0}^{n-1} a[r] * w[i+r],  i=0..n-1.
    This is the vector derivative part of dr a*a / da weighted by w.
    """
    a = _as_1d(a)
    w = _as_1d(w)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    need = 3 * n - 2
    L = 1
    while L < need:
        L <<= 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
    d = np.fft.irfft(np.conj(fa) * fw, L)
    d = d[:n]
    d = np.maximum(d, 0.0)
    return d


def _theta_from_x(x, n):
    th = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 0:
        return th
    if n == 1:
        th[0] = 0.0
        return th
    th[:-1] = x
    th[-1] = -float(np.sum(x, dtype=np.float64))
    return th


def _lbfbs_func_x(x, n, beta):
    """
    L-BFGS objective/gradient for log-parameter x of length n-1, sum(theta)=0.
    Minimizes a smooth approximation to max(conv(a,a)).
    """
    if n <= 1:
        return 0.0, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size != n - 1:
        return 1e6, np.zeros(max(0, n - 1), dtype=np.float64)
    th = _theta_from_x(x, n)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(th)):
        return 1e6, np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
    tm = float(np.max(th))
    aa = np.exp(th - tm)
    s = float(np.sum(aa))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return 1e6, np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
    aa = aa / s

    b = _self_convolution(aa)
    bm = float(np.max(b))
    if bm <= 1e-300:
        return 0.0, np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)

    lam = float(beta) / bm
    centered = b - bm
    ew = np.exp(lam * centered)
    sw = float(np.sum(ew))
    if (not np.isfinite(sw)) or sw <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros(b.size, dtype=np.float64)
        idx = int(np.argmax(b))
        w[idx] = 1.0
        internal = bm
    else:
        w = ew / sw
        internal = bm + float(np.log(sw)) / lam

    d = _cross_corr_a_w(aa, w)
    ad = float(np.dot(aa, d))
    grad_th = 2.0 * aa * (d - ad)
    grad_x = grad_th[:-1] - grad_th[-1]
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_x)):
        grad_x = np.zeros(n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(internal), grad_x


def _lbfbs_budget(n, time_left):
    if n <= 128:
        if time_left > 40.0:
            return 3500, 800
        if time_left > 20.0:
            return 2000, 500
        return 1000, 250
    if n <= 512:
        if time_left > 80.0:
            return 2500, 700
        if time_left > 40.0:
            return 1200, 350
        return 600, 200
    if time_left > 120.0:
        return 2500, 700
    if time_left > 60.0:
        return 1200, 350
    return 500, 150


def _lbfbs_polish(a, n, beta, maxfun, maxiter):
    a = _normalize(a)
    if n <= 1:
        return a
    if not _HAVE_SCIPY:
        return a
    th = np.log(a + 1e-300)
    th = th - float(np.mean(th))
    x = th[:n - 1]
    try:
        res = _opt.minimize(
            _lbfbs_func_x,
            x,
            args=(n, beta),
            jac=True,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            options={"maxiter": maxiter, "maxfun": maxfun, "ftol": 1e-12, "gtol": 1e-7},
        )
        if res is not None and res.x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
            th2 = _theta_from_x(res.x, n)
            th2 = th2 - float(np.max(th2))
            out = np.exp(th2)
            s = float(np.sum(out))
            if np.isfinite(s) and s > 1e-300:
                out = out / s
                out = np.clip(out, 0.0, None)
                return _normalize(out)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return a


def _fourier_array(n, rng, mode):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    if mode == 0:
        alpha = float(rng.uniform(-0.8, 0.8))
        p = 1.0 + alpha * (2.0 * x - 1.0)
    elif mode == 1:
        K = int(rng.integers(3, 8))
        v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for m in range(1, K + 1):
            amp = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0 / (m + 0.4)))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            v += amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * m * x + ph)
        sd = float(np.std(v))
        if sd > 1e-12:
            v = v * float(rng.uniform(0.35, 1.6)) / sd
        else:
            v = rng.normal(0.0, 0.5, size=n)
        p = np.exp(v)
    elif mode == 2:
        K = int(rng.integers(3, 8))
        v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for m in range(1, K + 1):
            amp = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0 / (m + 0.4)))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            v += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * x + ph)
        sd = float(np.std(v))
        if sd > 1e-12:
            v = v * float(rng.uniform(0.35, 1.6)) / sd
        else:
            v = rng.normal(0.0, 0.5, size=n)
        p = np.exp(v)
    elif mode == 3:
        alpha = float(rng.uniform(-0.4, 3.0))
        beta = float(rng.uniform(-0.4, 3.0))
        eps = 0.02
        p = (x + eps) ** alpha * ((1.0 - x) + eps) ** beta
    else:
        A1 = float(rng.uniform(0.25, 1.05))
        A2 = float(rng.uniform(0.10, 0.65))
        ph1 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        ph2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        f1 = int(rng.integers(1, 4))
        f2 = int(rng.integers(2, 9))
        p = 1.0 + A1 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f1 * x + ph1) + A2 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f2 * x + ph2)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.clip(p, 1e-9, None)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 1e-300:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(n)
    return p / s


def _resample_sequence(a, n):
    a = _normalize(a)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == n:
        return a
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    li = np.interp(x1, x0, loga)
    li = li - float(np.max(li))
    out = np.exp(li)
    s = float(np.sum(out))
    if s <= 1e-300:
        out = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(n)
    out = out / s
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, None)
    return _normalize(out)


def _make_initial_candidates(n, seed, parent_seq=None, max_candidates=80, time_limit=5.0):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    raw = []
    counter = 0

    def _add(p):
        nonlocal counter
        if p is None:
            return
        p = _normalize(p)
        if p.size != n:
            p = _resample_sequence(p, n)
        sc = _score(p)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            raw.append((float(sc), counter, p))
            counter += 1

    if parent_seq is not None:
        arr = _as_1d(parent_seq)
        if arr.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
            _add(_resample_sequence(arr, n))

    for mode in range(5):
        _add(_fourier_array(n, rng, mode))

    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(0.0, float(time_limit))
    while len(raw) < max_candidates and time.monotonic() < deadline:
        mode = int(rng.integers(0, 5))
        _add(_fourier_array(n, rng, mode))

    raw.sort()
    out = []
    for sc, _, p in raw:
        out.append((sc, p))
    return out


def _lp_candidate(a):
    if not _HAVE_SCIPY:
        return None
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = a.size
    if n > 200:
        return None
    b = _self_convolution(a)
    M = float(np.max(b))
    if M <= 1e-300:
        return None
    m = 2 * n - 1
    T = np.zeros((m, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for x in range(m):
        start = max(0, x - n + 1)
        stop = min(n - 1, x)
        if stop >= start:
            idx = np.arange(start, stop + 1, dtype=np.int64)
            T[x, idx] = a[x - idx]
    c = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n
    res = None
    try:
        res = _opt.linprog(c, A_ub=T, b_ub=M, bounds=bounds, method="highs")
    except Exception:
        res = None
    if res is None:
        try:
            res = _opt.linprog(c, A_ub=T, b_ub=M, bounds=bounds)
        except Exception:
            return None
    try:
        if res.success:
            g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
            g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
            g = np.clip(g, 0.0, None)
            s = float(np.sum(g))
            if s > 1e-300:
                return _normalize(g)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None


def _lp_blend_candidate(a):
    a = _normalize(a)
    base_sc = _score(a)
    best = (a, float(base_sc))
    g = _lp_candidate(a)
    if g is None:
        return best
    ts = (0.1, 0.3, 0.5, 0.7, 1.0)
    for t in ts:
        cand = (1.0 - t) * a + t * g
        cand = _normalize(cand)
        sc = _score(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best[1]:
            best = (cand, float(sc))
    return best


def _step_values_for(n):
    if n <= 128:
        return (0.05, 0.2, 0.8, 2.0)
    if n <= 1024:
        return (0.02, 0.1, 0.4, 1.2)
    return (0.01, 0.05, 0.2, 0.8)


def _smooth_candidate(a, p, step_values):
    a = _normalize(a)
    b = _self_convolution(a)
    bm = float(np.max(b))
    base_sc = 2.0 * a.size * bm
    best = (a, float(base_sc))
    if bm <= 1e-300:
        return best
    y = np.maximum(b / bm, 0.0)
    w = np.power(y, float(p))
    sw = float(np.sum(w))
    if not np.isfinite(sw) or sw <= 1e-300:
        return best
    w = w / sw
    d = _cross_corr_a_w(a, w)
    if d.size == 0:
        return best
    mean_d = float(np.mean(d))
    direction = mean_d - d
    scale = float(np.max(np.abs(direction)))
    if scale < 1e-15:
        return best
    loga = np.log(a + 1e-300)
    normalized_dir = direction / scale
    for t in step_values:
        cand = np.exp(loga + float(t) * normalized_dir)
        cand = np.where(np.isfinite(cand), cand, 0.0)
        cand = np.clip(cand, 0.0, None)
        s = float(np.sum(cand))
        if s <= 1e-300:
            continue
        cand = cand / s
        sc = _score(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best[1]:
            best = (cand, float(sc))
    return best


def _block_candidate(a, rng):
    a = _normalize(a)
    cand = a.copy()
    n = a.size
    u = rng.random()
    if u < 0.3:
        width = int(rng.integers(2, max(3, min(n, 16))))
        sigma = float(rng.uniform(0.02, 0.25))
    elif u < 0.7:
        width = int(rng.integers(max(3, n // 20), max(4, n // 5)))
        sigma = float(rng.uniform(0.03, 0.30))
    else:
        width = int(rng.integers(max(4, n // 10), max(5, n // 2)))
        sigma = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.55))
    if width >= n:
        start = 0
        width = n
    else:
        start = int(rng.integers(0, n - width + 1))
    deltas = rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=width)
    cand[start:start + width] *= np.exp(deltas)
    if rng.random() < 0.2:
        zwidth = max(1, width // 4)
        zstart = start + int(rng.integers(0, max(1, width - zwidth + 1)))
        cand[zstart:zstart + zwidth] = 0.0
    cand = _normalize(cand)
    return cand, _score(cand)


def _coord_candidate(a, rng):
    a = _normalize(a)
    cand = a.copy()
    n = a.size
    idx = int(rng.integers(0, n))
    factors = (0.0, 0.2, 0.5, 0.8, 1.25, 1.6, 2.5, 5.0)
    f = float(rng.choice(factors))
    cand[idx] *= f
    cand = _normalize(cand)
    return cand, _score(cand)


def _perturb_log(a, sigma, rng):
    a = _normalize(a)
    loga = np.log(a + 1e-300)
    loga += rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=loga.size)
    loga = loga - float(np.max(loga))
    out = np.exp(loga)
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    return _normalize(out)


def _local_search(a, seed, deadline, allow_lp=False, lp_interval=400, lp_max=5):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    a = _normalize(a)
    cur = a.copy()
    cur_sc = _score(cur)
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = cur_sc

    steps = 0
    no_improve = 0
    lp_calls = 0
    T = 0.012
    step_values = _step_values_for(a.size)
    p_options = (1, 2, 3, 5, 8, 13, 21, 34)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        steps += 1

        if allow_lp and lp_calls < lp_max and (steps % max(1, int(lp_interval)) == 0):
            cand_sc = _lp_blend_candidate(cur)
            lp_calls += 1
        else:
            u = rng.random()
            if u < 0.55:
                if best_sc > 1.75:
                    half = max(1, len(p_options) // 2)
                    p = int(p_options[int(rng.integers(0, half))])
                else:
                    half = max(1, len(p_options) // 2)
                    p = int(p_options[int(rng.integers(half, len(p_options)))])
                cand_sc = _smooth_candidate(cur, p, step_values)
            elif u < 0.80:
                cand_sc = _block_candidate(cur, rng)
            else:
                cand_sc = _coord_candidate(cur, rng)

        cand = cand_sc[0]
        sc = cand_sc[1]
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            continue

        if sc < cur_sc - 1e-12:
            cur = cand
            cur_sc = sc
            no_improve = 0
            if sc < best_sc:
                best = cand.copy()
                best_sc = sc
                T = max(1e-6, T * 0.985)
            T = max(1e-6, T * 0.998)
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if T > 1e-12 and (sc - cur_sc) < 0.05:
                prob = np.exp(-(sc - cur_sc) / T)
                if rng.random() < prob:
                    cur = cand
                    cur_sc = sc

        if steps % 1000 == 0:
            print("    local step", steps, "best", round(float(best_sc), 8),
                  "cur", round(float(cur_sc), 8), flush=True)

        if no_improve > 450:
            restart = _perturb_log(best, 0.10, rng)
            restart_sc = _score(restart)
            if np.isfinite(restart_sc) and restart_sc < best_sc + 0.12:
                cur = restart
                cur_sc = restart_sc
            else:
                cur = best.copy()
                cur_sc = best_sc
            no_improve = 0
            T = 0.005

    return best, best_sc


def _low_res_search(n, seed, deadline, parent_seq=None):
    inits = _make_initial_candidates(
        n, seed, parent_seq,
        max_candidates=80,
        time_limit=max(0.2, min(4.0, max(0.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.10)))
    )
    if len(inits) == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)

    best_a = inits[0][1]
    best_sc = inits[0][0]
    print("  low-res initial best:", round(float(best_sc), 8), flush=True)

    if _HAVE_SCIPY and n <= 200:
        cand_sc = _lp_blend_candidate(best_a)
        if np.isfinite(cand_sc[1]) and cand_sc[1] < best_sc:
            best_a = cand_sc[0]
            best_sc = cand_sc[1]

    top_count = min(3, len(inits))
    betas = (8, 30, 70)
    for idx in range(top_count):
        init_a = inits[idx][1]
        for beta in betas:
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 5.0:
                break
            time_left = deadline - time.monotonic()
            maxfun, maxiter = _lbfbs_budget(n, time_left)
            polished = _lbfbs_polish(init_a, n, beta, maxfun, maxiter)
            sc = _score(polished)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_a = polished
                best_sc = sc
                print("  low-res L-BFGS beta", beta, "score", round(float(sc), 8), flush=True)

    a, sc = _local_search(
        best_a,
        seed + 99999,
        deadline,
        allow_lp=(_HAVE_SCIPY and n <= 200),
        lp_interval=180,
        lp_max=5,
    )
    print("  low-res local best:", round(float(sc), 8), flush=True)
    return a


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=400.0, **kwargs):
    """Return the best non-negative height sequence found within the time budget."""
    start_t = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 400.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 0.0:
        budget = 400.0
    total_time = max(1.0, budget - 5.0)
    deadline = start_t + total_time

    try:
        raw_parent = height_sequence_1
    except Exception:
        raw_parent = None

    parent_seq = None
    if raw_parent is not None:
        parent_seq = _as_1d(raw_parent)
        if parent_seq.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(parent_seq)):
            parent_seq = None

    if total_time >= 120.0:
        n_high = 1024
        n_low = 128
    elif total_time >= 60.0:
        n_high = 512
        n_low = 128
    else:
        n_high = 256
        n_low = 64

    best_a = None
    best_sc = float("inf")

    if total_time > 35.0 and n_low >= 48:
        low_budget = min(75.0, max(15.0, 0.25 * total_time))
        low_deadline = start_t + low_budget
        low_a = _low_res_search(n_low, int(seed), low_deadline, parent_seq)
        if low_a is not None:
            high_a = _resample_sequence(low_a, n_high)
            sc = _score(high_a)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                best_a = high_a
                best_sc = sc
                print("initial high from low-res:", round(float(sc), 8), flush=True)

    init_time = max(0.2, min(5.0, 0.03 * total_time))
    high_inits = _make_initial_candidates(
        n_high,
        int(seed) + 777,
        parent_seq,
        max_candidates=70,
        time_limit=init_time,
    )
    for sc, a in high_inits[:6]:
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_a = a
            best_sc = sc

    if best_a is None or not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        best_a = np.ones(n_high, dtype=np.float64) / float(n_high)
        best_sc = _score(best_a)

    print("best before high local:", round(float(best_sc), 8), flush=True)

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 3.0:
        best_a, best_sc = _local_search(best_a, int(seed) + 12345, deadline, allow_lp=False)
        print("best after high local:", round(float(best_sc), 8), flush=True)

    time_left = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if time_left > 55.0:
        maxfun, maxiter = _lbfbs_budget(n_high, time_left)
        polished = _lbfbs_polish(best_a, n_high, 40, maxfun, maxiter)
        sc = _score(polished)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_a = polished
            best_sc = sc
            print("high L-BFGS polish:", round(float(sc), 8), flush=True)
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 15.0:
            best_a, best_sc = _local_search(
                best_a,
                int(seed) + 54321,
                min(deadline, time.monotonic() + 20.0),
                allow_lp=False,
            )

    # Optional larger-resolution attempt if we are still above the benchmark.
    if best_sc > 1.5027 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 115.0:
        n_large = 2048
        a_large = _resample_sequence(best_a, n_large)
        sc_large = _score(a_large)
        if np.isfinite(sc_large):
            dl = time.monotonic() + min(75.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 12.0)
            if dl > time.monotonic():
                a_large, sc_large = _local_search(a_large, int(seed) + 2718, dl, allow_lp=False)
            if np.isfinite(sc_large) and sc_large < best_sc:
                best_a = a_large
                best_sc = sc_large
                print("larger resolution improved:", round(float(sc_large), 8), flush=True)

    final_list = [float(v) for v in best_a]
    # Basic sanity clean-up.
    if not final_list:
        final_list = [1.0 / 1024.0] * 1024
    cleaned = []
    sumv = 0.0
    for v in final_list:
        if not np.isfinite(v) or v < 0.0:
            v = 0.0
        if v > 1000.0:
            v = 1000.0
        cleaned.append(float(v))
        sumv += float(v)
    if sumv <= 0.01:
        cleaned = [1.0 / 1024.0] * 1024

    # Final authoritative check, if available and if there is enough time.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 1.5:
        try:
            exact = evaluate_sequence(cleaned)
            print("final evaluate_sequence score:", exact, flush=True)
            if not np.isfinite(exact):
                cleaned = [1.0 / 1024.0] * 1024
        except Exception:
            pass

    return cleaned
```
